FILE: lib/products.ts:6

export async function getProducts() {
  const r = await fetch(API, { cache: "no-store" });
  return await r.json();
}

export async function getProduct(id: string) {
  const products = await getProducts();
  return products.find((p: any) => p.id === id || p.slug === id);
}

export async function getProductById(id: string) {
  return getProduct(id);
}


FILE: lib/products.ts:26

export async function saveProduct(product: any) {
  const r = await fetch(API, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(product),
  });

  return await r.json();
}

FILE: lib/products.ts:36

export async function updateProduct(id: string, product: any) {
  const r = await fetch(API, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ ...product, id }),
  });

  return await r.json();
}

    method: "DELETE",

FILE: lib/products.ts:46

export async function deleteProduct(id: string) {
  const r = await fetch(API, {
    method: "DELETE",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ id }),
  });

  return await r.json();
}

FILE: lib/importer/ProductImporter.ts.bak:17
export class ProductImporter {
  static async loadProducts(): Promise<ImportedProduct[]> {
    const res = await fetch("/products.json");

    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error("Unable to load products.");
    }

    return (await res.json()) as ImportedProduct[];
  }
}

FILE: lib/importer/ProductImporter.ts:17
export class ProductImporter {
  static async loadProducts(): Promise<ImportedProduct[]> {
    const res = await fetch("/api/cms/products");

    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error("Unable to load products.");
    }

    return (await res.json()) as ImportedProduct[];
  }
}

export async function getOrders() {

  const res = await fetch(
    "/api/admin/orders"
  );

  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error("Failed to load orders");
  }

  return res.json();
